Ohio (OH) LEADS
Transport: TCPIP ­ DMPP-2020
Routing and Configuration Information: Save Byte Routing

Disclaimer Statement

 The LEADS assigned ORI for a device (or for the site) should be placed in the <Authentication>/<ORI> field in the ConnectCIC header
 The assigned Drivers License Number should be placed in the <Authentication>/<UserName> field in the ConnectCIC header
 If applicable, the LEADS assigned Terminal ID can be placed in the <Authentication>/<Mnemonic> field in the ConnectCIC header
 <Authentication>/<OriginatingAgencyORI> can be populated with an ORI different from value placed in <Authentication>/<ORI> if a terminal is making a request (Query only) on behalf of another ORI. Due to policy and technological restraints, this value is not used for Entry/Locate/Clear/Cancel/Modify or BMV image lookup transactions.
Security Requirements: Ohio has strict requirements for agencies to complete prior to being allowed access on the LEADS switch. Agencies must complete a security plan including physical and network security. Agencies should contact LEADS for details early in the process to allow enough time to complete the plan and obtain approval from the state.
Logon Requirements: For the LEADS Ohio Enhanced Agency interface, agencies will be required to submit a USER-ID for each transaction. This field is variable in length with a maximum of 30 ASCII alpha-numeric characters and is the assigned identifier for the user operating the device originating the transaction. This will need to be submitted as part of the header. Note: This field is currently being defined by the State of Ohio and the agencies will need to identify what they will be using for this field.
Certification Requirements: None Need Site Sponsor
Data-Mined Transactions & Fields: NCIC (QA, QB, QG, QV, QW) and DMV (Person and Vehicle) Tags returned from Data mining
